{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"gen-1783053852-zdt1DYTrnBrr4vm21oRt","type":"message","role":"assistant","container":null,"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"functions.Write:9","caller":{"type":"direct"},"name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/fill_feedback.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nFill in training_feedback_template.hwpx using values from training_feedback.json,\nhandling fragmented placeholders and repackaging into a valid HWPX.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport shutil\nimport zipfile\n\n\ndef korean_number_to_digits(text):\n    \"\"\"Convert Korean number words to Arabic digits.\"\"\"\n    # Remove any Arabic digits first and count them\n    arabic_match = re.search(r'\\d+', text)\n    if arabic_match:\n        return arabic_match.group()\n\n    # Korean numeral mapping\n    units = {\n        '일': 1, '이': 2, '삼': 3, '사': 4, '오': 5,\n        '육': 6, '칠': 7, '팔': 8, '구': 9,\n    }\n    tens_map = {\n        '십': 10, '백': 100, '천': 1000,\n    }\n    big_units = {\n        '만': 10000, '억': 100000000,\n    }\n\n    # Simple mapping for common pure Korean numbers\n    pure_korean = {\n        '하나': 1, '한': 1, '둘': 2, '두': 2, '셋': 3, '세': 3,\n        '넷': 4, '네': 4, '다섯': 5, '여섯': 6, '일곱': 7,\n        '여덟': 8, '아홉': 9, '열': 10, '열한': 11, '열두': 12,\n        '열셋': 13, '열네': 14, '열다섯': 15, '열여섯': 16,\n        '열일곱': 17, '열여덟': 18, '열아홉': 19,\n        '스물': 20, '스물한': 21, '스물두': 22, '스물셋': 23,\n        '스물네': 24, '스물다섯': 25, '스물여섯': 26, '스물일곱': 27,\n        '스물여덟': 28, '스물아홉': 29,\n        '서른': 30, '마흔': 40, '쉰': 50, '예순': 60, '일흔': 70,\n        '여든': 80, '아흔': 90,\n    }\n\n    text = text.strip()\n    # Try pure Korean first\n    for word, num in sorted(pure_korean.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):\n        if word in text:\n            return str(num)\n\n    # Try Sino-Korean parsing\n    def parse_sinokorean(s):\n        result = 0\n        temp = 0\n        i = 0\n        while i < len(s):\n            matched = False\n            # Check big units\n            for k, v in sorted(big_units.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):\n                if s.startswith(k, i):\n                    if temp == 0:\n                        temp = 1\n                    result += temp * v\n                    temp = 0\n                    i += len(k)\n                    matched = True\n                    break\n            if matched:\n                continue\n            # Check tens_map\n            for k, v in sorted(tens_map.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):\n                if s.startswith(k, i):\n                    if temp == 0:\n                        temp = 1\n                    temp *= v\n                    result += temp\n                    temp = 0\n                    i += len(k)\n                    matched = True\n                    break\n            if matched:\n                continue\n            # Check units\n            for k, v in sorted(units.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):\n                if s.startswith(k, i):\n                    temp = temp * 10 + v if temp else v\n                    i += len(k)\n                    matched = True\n                    break\n            if not matched:\n                i += 1\n        return result + temp\n\n    val = parse_sinokorean(text)\n    if val > 0:\n        return str(val)\n\n    return text\n\n\ndef transform_values(data):\n    \"\"\"Apply business-logic transformations to JSON values.\"\"\"\n    transformed = {}\n    for key, value in data.items():\n        if key == '참석자수':\n            transformed[key] = korean_number_to_digits(value)\n        elif key == '만족도':\n            # Extract numeric score\n            m = re.search(r'(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)', str(value))\n            if m:\n                score = m.group(1)\n                transformed[key] = f\"{score}점 (5.0점 만점)\"\n            else:\n                transformed[key] = value\n        elif key == '종합의견':\n            transformed[key] = value + \" 후속 심화반 검토 요망.\"\n        else:\n            transformed[key] = value\n    return transformed\n\n\ndef consolidate_placeholders(xml_text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Consolidate fragmented placeholders like:\n      <hp:t>{{</hp:t><hp:t>key</hp:t><hp:t>}}</hp:t>\n    into:\n      <hp:t>{{key}}</hp:t>\n    Removes empty siblings and their parent runs if they become empty.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pattern to find adjacent <hp:t> elements that together form {{...}}\n    # We do this iteratively until no more changes.\n    changed = True\n    while changed:\n        changed = False\n        # Match sequences of <hp:t>...</hp:t> inside <hp:run> or directly inside <hp:p>\n        # Look for patterns where consecutive <hp:t> elements contain {{, key, }}\n        # A simpler approach: find all <hp:t> blocks and merge contiguous ones inside the same parent\n        # that together match the placeholder pattern.\n\n        # Strategy: replace patterns like:\n        #   <hp:t>{{</hp:t></hp:run><hp:run...><hp:t>KEY</hp:t></hp:run><hp:run...><hp:t>}}</hp:t>\n        # or within the same run.\n        # We can use regex to find the whole fragmented placeholder across tags.\n\n        # Broad regex to capture fragmented {{...}}\n        pattern = re.compile(\n            r'<hp:t>\\{\\{</hp:t>(?:</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>)?\\s*'\n            r'<hp:t>([^<]+)</hp:t>(?:</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>)?\\s*'\n            r'<hp:t>\\}\\}</hp:t>',\n            re.S\n        )\n        if pattern.search(xml_text):\n            xml_text = pattern.sub(r'<hp:t>{{\\1}}</hp:t>', xml_text)\n            changed = True\n\n        # Also handle more fragmentation: each char in separate <hp:t>\n        # e.g. <hp:t>{</hp:t><hp:t>{</hp:t>...<hp:t>}</hp:t><hp:t>}</hp:t>\n        pattern2 = re.compile(\n            r'<hp:t>\\{</hp:t>\\s*<hp:t>\\{</hp:t>(?:</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>)?\\s*'\n            r'<hp:t>([^<]+)</hp:t>(?:</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>)?\\s*'\n            r'<hp:t>\\}</hp:t>\\s*<hp:t>\\}</hp:t>',\n            re.S\n        )\n        if pattern2.search(xml_text):\n            xml_text = pattern2.sub(r'<hp:t>{{\\1}}</hp:t>', xml_text)\n            changed = True\n\n    return xml_text\n\n\ndef remove_linesegarray_from_modified_paragraphs(xml_text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Remove <hp:linesegarray>...</hp:linesegarray> from paragraphs whose text was modified.\n    We can't easily know which paragraphs were modified, so we remove from ALL paragraphs\n    that contain replaced content. Simpler: remove from all paragraphs that don't contain\n    any placeholders and had text changes... but since we do string replacement, we can\n    just check if the paragraph still has '{{' in it or not.\n\n    Actually, a safer approach: remove <hp:linesegarray> from ANY paragraph that\n    does NOT contain a placeholder after replacement (i.e., all paragraphs except those\n    that still have un-replaced placeholders). But since we should have no placeholders\n    left, we can just strip all of them, or more conservatively:\n    strip them only from paragraphs where the text was likely modified.\n\n    Let's do: for each <hp:p>...</hp:p>, if it had a placeholder that was replaced,\n    remove its <hp:linesegarray>. We approximate this by checking if the paragraph\n    originally contained '{{'.\n    \"\"\"\n    def process_para(m):\n        para = m.group(0)\n        if '{{' in para:\n            # This paragraph had a placeholder, remove linesegarray\n            para = re.sub(r'<hp:linesegarray>.*?</hp:linesegarray>', '', para, flags=re.S)\n        return para\n\n    return re.sub(r'<hp:p\\b[^>]*>.*?</hp:p>', process_para, xml_text, flags=re.S)\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(filepath, replacements):\n    \"\"\"Read XML, consolidate placeholders, replace, clean layout cache, write back.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n\n    original = content\n\n    # Step 1: consolidate fragmented placeholders\n    content = consolidate_placeholders(content)\n\n    # Step 2: simple string replacements\n    for key, value in replacements.items():\n        placeholder = '{{' + key + '}}'\n        content = content.replace(placeholder, value)\n\n    # Step 3: remove layout cache from modified paragraphs\n    content = remove_linesegarray_from_modified_paragraphs(content)\n\n    if content != original:\n        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            f.write(content)\n\n    return content\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Paths\n    json_path = '/root/training_feedback.json'\n    template_path = '/root/training_feedback_template.hwpx'\n    output_path = '/root/training_feedback_ready.hwpx'\n    work_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_work_ready'\n\n    # Clean and extract\n    if os.path.exists(work_dir):\n        shutil.rmtree(work_dir)\n    os.makedirs(work_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(template_path, 'r') as zin:\n        zin.extractall(work_dir)\n\n    # Read and transform JSON\n    with open(json_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    replacements = transform_values(data)\n\n    print(\"Replacements:\")\n    for k, v in replacements.items():\n        print(f\"  {k} -> {v}\")\n\n    # Check big units\n            for k, v in sorted(big_units.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):\n                if s.startswith(k, i):\n                    if temp == 0:\n                        temp = 1\n                    result += temp * v\n                    temp = 0\n                    i += len(k)\n                    matched = True\n                    break\n            if matched:\n                continue\n            # Check tens_map\n            for k, v in sorted(tens_map.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):\n                if s.startswith(k, i):\n                    if temp == 0:\n                        temp = 1\n                    temp *= v\n                    result += temp\n                    temp = 0\n                    i += len(k)\n                    matched = True\n                    break\n            if matched:\n                continue\n            # Check units\n            for k, v in sorted(units.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):\n                if s.startswith(k, i):\n                    temp = temp * 10 + v if temp else v\n                    i += len(k)\n                    matched = True\n                    break\n            if not matched:\n                i += 1\n        return result + temp\n\n    val = parse_sinokorean(text)\n    if val > 0:\n        return str(val)\n\n    return text\n\n\ndef transform_values(data):\n    \"\"\"Apply business-logic transformations to JSON values.\"\"\"\n    transformed = {}\n    for key, value in data.items():\n        if key == '참석자수':\n            transformed[key] = korean_number_to_digits(value)\n        elif key == '만족도':\n            # Extract numeric score\n            m = re.search(r'(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)', str(value))\n            if m:\n                score = m.group(1)\n                transformed[key] = f\"{score}점 (5.0점 만점)\"\n            else:\n                transformed[key] = value\n        elif key == '종합의견':\n            transformed[key] = value + \" 후속 심화반 검토 요망.\"\n        else:\n            transformed[key] = value\n    return transformed\n\n\ndef consolidate_placeholders(xml_text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Consolidate fragmented placeholders like:\n      <hp:t>{{</hp:t><hp:t>key</hp:t><hp:t>}}</hp:t>\n    into:\n      <hp:t>{{key}}</hp:t>\n    Removes empty siblings and their parent runs if they become empty.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Pattern to find adjacent <hp:t> elements that together form {{...}}\n    # We do this iteratively until no more changes.\n    changed = True\n    while changed:\n        changed = False\n        # Match sequences of <hp:t>...</hp:t> inside <hp:run> or directly inside <hp:p>\n        # Look for patterns where consecutive <hp:t> elements contain {{, key, }}\n        # A simpler approach: find all <hp:t> blocks and merge contiguous ones inside the same parent\n        # that together match the placeholder pattern.\n\n        # Strategy: replace patterns like:\n        #   <hp:t>{{</hp:t></hp:run><hp:run...><hp:t>KEY</hp:t></hp:run><hp:run...><hp:t>}}</hp:t>\n        # or within the same run.\n        # We can use regex to find the whole fragmented placeholder across tags.\n\n        # Broad regex to capture fragmented {{...}}\n        pattern = re.compile(\n            r'<hp:t>\\{\\{</hp:t>(?:</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>)?\\s*'\n            r'<hp:t>([^<]+)</hp:t>(?:</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>)?\\s*'\n            r'<hp:t>\\}\\}</hp:t>',\n            re.S\n        )\n        if pattern.search(xml_text):\n            xml_text = pattern.sub(r'<hp:t>{{\\1}}</hp:t>', xml_text)\n            changed = True\n\n        # Also handle more fragmentation: each char in separate <hp:t>\n        # e.g. <hp:t>{</hp:t><hp:t>{</hp:t>...<hp:t>}</hp:t><hp:t>}</hp:t>\n        pattern2 = re.compile(\n            r'<hp:t>\\{</hp:t>\\s*<hp:t>\\{</hp:t>(?:</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>)?\\s*'\n            r'<hp:t>([^<]+)</hp:t>(?:</hp:run>\\s*<hp:run[^>]*>)?\\s*'\n            r'<hp:t>\\}</hp:t>\\s*<hp:t>\\}</hp:t>',\n            re.S\n        )\n        if pattern2.search(xml_text):\n            xml_text = pattern2.sub(r'<hp:t>{{\\1}}</hp:t>', xml_text)\n            changed = True\n\n    return xml_text\n\n\ndef remove_linesegarray_from_modified_paragraphs(xml_text):\n    \"\"\"\n    Remove <hp:linesegarray>...</hp:linesegarray> from paragraphs whose text was modified.\n    We can't easily know which paragraphs were modified, so we remove from ALL paragraphs\n    that contain replaced content. Simpler: remove from all paragraphs that don't contain\n    any placeholders and had text changes... but since we do string replacement, we can\n    just check if the paragraph still has '{{' in it or not.\n\n    Actually, a safer approach: remove <hp:linesegarray> from ANY paragraph that\n    does NOT contain a placeholder after replacement (i.e., all paragraphs except those\n    that still have un-replaced placeholders). But since we should have no placeholders\n    left, we can just strip all of them, or more conservatively:\n    strip them only from paragraphs where the text was likely modified.\n\n    Let's do: for each <hp:p>...</hp:p>, if it had a placeholder that was replaced,\n    remove its <hp:linesegarray>. We approximate this by checking if the paragraph\n    originally contained '{{'.\n    \"\"\"\n    def process_para(m):\n        para = m.group(0)\n        if '{{' in para:\n            # This paragraph had a placeholder, remove linesegarray\n            para = re.sub(r'<hp:linesegarray>.*?</hp:linesegarray>', '', para, flags=re.S)\n        return para\n\n    return re.sub(r'<hp:p\\b[^>]*>.*?</hp:p>', process_para, xml_text, flags=re.S)\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(filepath, replacements):\n    \"\"\"Read XML, consolidate placeholders, replace, clean layout cache, write back.\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n\n    original = content\n\n    # Step 1: consolidate fragmented placeholders\n    content = consolidate_placeholders(content)\n\n    # Step 2: simple string replacements\n    for key, value in replacements.items():\n        placeholder = '{{' + key + '}}'\n        content = content.replace(placeholder, value)\n\n    # Step 3: remove layout cache from modified paragraphs\n    content = remove_linesegarray_from_modified_paragraphs(content)\n\n    if content != original:\n        with open(filepath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            f.write(content)\n\n    return content\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Paths\n    json_path = '/root/training_feedback.json'\n    template_path = '/root/training_feedback_template.hwpx'\n    output_path = '/root/training_feedback_ready.hwpx'\n    work_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_work_ready'\n\n    # Clean and extract\n    if os.path.exists(work_dir):\n        shutil.rmtree(work_dir)\n    os.makedirs(work_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(template_path, 'r') as zin:\n        zin.extractall(work_dir)\n\n    # Read and transform JSON\n    with open(json_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    replacements = transform_values(data)\n\n    print(\"Replacements:\")\n    for k, v in replacements.items():\n        print(f\"  {k} -> {v}\")\n\n    # Find and process all XML files in the archive\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(work_dir):\n        for fname in files:\n            if fname.endswith('.xml'):\n                fpath = os.path.join(root, fname)\n                process_xml_file(fpath, replacements)\n\n    # Repackage\n    if os.path.exists(output_path):\n        os.remove(output_path)\n\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(work_dir):\n            for fname in files:\n                fpath = os.path.join(root, fname)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(fpath, work_dir)\n                zout.write(fpath, arcname)\n\n    print(f\"\\nSaved to {output_path}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","numLines":273,"startLine":1,"totalLines":273}}}

Remove any Arabic digits first and count them\n    arabic_match = re.search(r'\\d+', text)\n    if arabic_match:\n        return arabic_match.group()\n\n    # Korean numeral mapping\n    units = {\n        '일': 1, '이': 2, '삼': 3, '사': 4, '오': 5,\n        '육': 6, '칠': 7, '팔': 8, '구': 9,\n    }\n    tens_map = {\n        '십': 10, '백': 100, '천': 1000,\n    }\n    big_units = {\n        '만': 10000, '억': 100000000,\n    }\n\n    # Simple mapping for common pure Korean numbers\n    pure_korean = {\n        '하나': 1, '한': 1, '둘': 2, '두': 2, '셋': 3, '세': 3,\n        '넷': 4, '네': 4, '다섯': 5, '여섯': 6, '일곱': 7,\n        '여덟': 8, '아홉': 9, '열': 10, '열한': 11, '열두': 12,\n        '열셋': 13, '열네': 14, '열다섯': 15, '열여섯': 16,\n        '열일곱': 17, '열여덟': 18, '열아홉': 19,\n        '스물': 20, '스물한': 21, '스물두': 22, '스물셋': 23,\n        '스물네': 24, '스물다섯': 25, '스물여섯': 26, '스물일곱': 27,\n        '스물여덟': 28, '스물아홉': 29,\n        '서른': 30, '마흔': 40, '쉰': 50, '예순': 60, '일흔': 70,\n        '여든': 80, '아흔': 90,\n    }\n\n    text = text.strip()\n    # Check big units\n            for k, v in sorted(big_units.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):\n                if s.startswith(k, i):\n                    if temp == 0:\n                        temp = 1\n                    result += temp * v\n                    temp = 0\n                    i += len(k)\n                    matched = True\n                    break\n            if matched:\n                continue\n            # Check tens_map\n            for k, v in sorted(tens_map.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[0])):\n                if s.startswith(k, i):\n                    if temp == 0:\n                        temp = 1\n                    temp *= v\n                    result += temp\n                    temp = 0\n                    i += len(k)\n                    matched = True\n                    break\n            if matched:\n                continue\n            #
